DISCLAIMER

In view of the stringent time requirements encountered by the Department of Basic Education to effect the necessary editorial changes and layout
to the Curriculum and Assessment Policy Statements and the supplementary policy documents, possible errors may occur in the said documents
placed on the official departmental websites.

There may also be vernacular inconsistencies in the language documents at Home-, First and Second Additional Language levels which have been
translated in the various African Languages. Please note that the content of the documents translated and versioned in the African Languages are
correct as they are based on the English generic language documents at all three language levels to be implemented in all four school phases.

If any editorial, layout or vernacular inconsistencies are detected, the user is kindly requested to bring this to the attention of the Department of
Basic Education.

 2 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
FOREWORD BY THE MINISTER

 Our national curriculum is the culmination of our efforts over a period of seventeen
 years to transform the curriculum bequeathed to us by apartheid. From the start of
 democracy we have built our curriculum on the values that inspired our Constitution
 (Act 108 of 1996). The Preamble to the Constitution states that the aims of the
 Constitution are to:

 • heal the divisions of the past and establish a society based on democratic
 values, social justice and fundamental human rights;

 • improve the quality of life of all citizens and free the potential of each person;

 • lay the foundations for a democratic and open society in which government is
 based on the will of the people and every citizen is equally protected by law;
 and

• build a united and democratic South Africa able to take its rightful place as a sovereign state in the family of
 nations.

Education and the curriculum have an important role to play in realising these aims.

In 1997 we introduced outcomes-based education to overcome the curricular divisions of the past, but the experience
of implementation prompted a review in 2000. This led to the first curriculum revision: the Revised National Curriculum
Statement Grades R-9 and the National Curriculum Statement Grades 10-12 (2002).

Ongoing implementation challenges resulted in another review in 2009 and we revised the Revised National
Curriculum Statement (2002) and the National Curriculum Statement Grades 10-12 to produce this document.

From 2012 the two National Curriculum Statements, for Grades R-9 and Grades 10-12 respectively, are combined
in a single document and will simply be known as the National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12. The National
Curriculum Statement for Grades R-12 builds on the previous curriculum but also updates it and aims to provide
clearer specification of what is to be taught and learnt on a term-by-term basis.

The National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12 represents a policy statement for learning and teaching in South
African schools and comprises of the following:

(a) Curriculum and Assessment Policy Statements (CAPS) for all approved subjects listed in this document;

(b) National policy pertaining to the programme and promotion requirements of the National Curriculum Statement
 Grades R-12; and

(c) National Protocol for Assessment Grades R-12.

MRS ANGIE MOTSHEKGA, MP
MINISTER OF BASIC EDUCATION

 2 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)

WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA

1.1 ISENDLALELO

IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke amaGreyidi R-12 (TKZ) sendlala umgomo wekharikhyulamu nokuhlola
emkhakheni wezokufunda.

Ukuthuthukisa ukusetjenziswa kwawo, isiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke satjhugululwa, amatjhuguluko la
azokuthoma ukusetjenziswa ngenyanga kaTjhirhweni ngomnyaka we-2012. Enziwa umtlolo owodwa opheleleko
womThetho-kambiso weKharikhyulamu nokuHlola esifundweni ngasinye ukujamiselela iinTatimende zeemFundo,
imiHlahlandlela yamaHlelo wokuFunda begodu nemiHlahlandlela yokuHlola iimFundo emaGreyidi ni R-12 .

1.2 UMBONO-MAZOMBE

(a) IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke seGreyidi R-12 (Sango Tjhirhweni we-2012) sijamele Isitatimende
 somThetho-kambiso wesitatimende sokufunda nokufundisa eenkolweni zeSewula Afrika begodu simumethe
 okulandelako:

 (i) IinTatimende zomThetho-kambiso weKharikhyulamu nokuHlola zaleso naleso sifundo sesikolo
 esiphasisweko

 (ii) Umtlolo womThetho-kambiso, National policy pertaining to the programme and promotion requirements
 of the National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12 ; begodu no

 (iii) Mtlolo womThetho-kambiso, iNational Protocol for Assessment Grades R-12 (Tjhirhweni 2012)

(b) IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke samaGreyidi R-12 (kaTjhirhweni 2011 sijamiselela iintatimende
 zekharikhyulamu zelizweloke ezimbili zanje, ezibizwa

 (i) Isitatimende seKarikhyulamu esiBuyekeziweko amaGreyidi R-9, Government Gazette No. 23406
 yamhlana ama-31 Mrhayili 2002, begodu ne-

 (ii) Isitatimende seKarikhyulamu seLizwe Loke amaGreyidi 10-12 Government Gazettes, No. 25545
 yamhlana amalanga asi-6 October 2003 and No. 27594 yangomhla we-17 kuMrhayili 2005.

(c) Iintatimende zekharikhyulamu yelizweloke eziseengatjaneni b (i) no (ii) ngehla, zimumethe imitlolo yemithetho-
 kambiso elandelako esuswe beyajanyiselelwa siTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke samaGreyidi 10-12
 (Tjhirhweni 2012) ngesikhathi seminyaka we-2012-2014.

 (i) IinTatimende zeemFundo, imiHlahlandlela yamaHlelo wokuFundo begodu nemihlahlandlela yokuHlola
 iimFundo emaGreyidi ni R-9 nemaGreyidi ni 10-12 ;

 (ii) Umtlolo womthetho-kambiso, iNational Policy on assessment and qualifications for schools in the
 General Education and Training Band, egadangiswe kuGovernment Notice No. 124 ku Government
 Gazette No. 29626 yamhlana amalanga ali- 12 kuMhlolanja 2007;

 umsebenzi womntwana bona ibe khona eenkolweni zomphakathi nezizijameleko.

1.3 IMINQOPHO EVAMILEKO YEKHARIKHYULAMU YESEWULA AFRIKA

(a) IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke samaGreyidi R-12 sethula lokho okungathathwa njengelwazi,
 amakghono namagugu okumele kufundwe eenkolweni zeSewula Afrika. Ikharikhyulamu le inqophe
 ukuqinisekisa bonyana abantwana bazuza bebasebenzise ilwazi namakghono ngeendlela ezizwisekako
 emaphilweni wabo. Ngalokho, ikharikhyulamu ithuthukisa umqondo wokubuthelela ilwazi ebujameni bendawo
 leyo, ngesikhathi atjheja ukukateleleka eembopweni zephasi.

(b) IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke amaGreyidi R-12 sifeza iminqopho yoku:

 • hlomisa abafundi, ngaphandle kokutjheja ibhoduluko lezokuhlalisana-nezomnotho, ubulili, ikghono
 lezomzimba nofana lezengqondo, amakghono namagugu adingekako ukuzanelisa, begodu
 nokuzibandakanya okuzwisisekako emphakathini njengabahlali benarha etjhaphulukileko;

 • ukwenza ifundo yamazinga aphezulu ifinyeleleke;

 • ukwenza kube lula bonyana abafundi basuke emazikweni wezefundo baye emisebenzini; begodu

 • kunikela abaqatjhi ngephrofayili eyaneleko yamakghono womfundi.

(c) IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke samaGreyidi R-12 sisekelwe eenkambisweni ezilandelako:

 • Ukutjhuguluka kwezokuhlalisana : ukuqinisekisa bonyana ukungalingani kezefundo kwesikhathi
 esidlulileko kwalungiswa nokuthi amathuba alinganako wezefundo anikelwe zoke iingaba zesintu.

 • Ukufunda ngomdlandla nangelihlo elihlabako : ukukhuthaza indlela yokufunda ngelihlo elihlabako
 nelinomdlandla, kunokobana kufundwe amaqiniso anikelweko ngehloko ngaphandle kokuwahlaba.

 4 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
 • Ilwazi namakghono asezingeni eliphakamileko : iimfuneko ezimbalwa zelwazi namakghono okufanele
 zifunyanwe kileyo naleyogreyidi, zibekiwe begodu zibekwe amazingeni aphakamileko, afunyanekako kizo
 zoke iimfundo.

 • Ukuragela phambili : okumumethweko nobujamo baleyo naleyo greyidi kutjengisa ukuragela phambili
 ukusukela kokulula ukuya kokubudisi.

(d) Amalungelo wobuntu, ukufaka koke, ubulungiswa bezebhoduluko nokuhlalisana : ukufaka imigomo
 yobulungiswa kezokuhlalisana, kezebhoduluko begodu namalungelo wabantu njengombana kuhlathululiwe
 kumThetho-sisekelo weSewula Afrika. IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke amaGreyidi 10-12 itjhetjha
 khulu izinto ezifana nokutlhaga, ukungafani, ukungalingani, ubuhlanga, ilimi, iminyaka, abaphila nokukhubazeka
 begodu namanye amatshwayo.

 • Ukukarekela amahlelo welwazi lobugugu bendabuko : ukuthabela umlando onothileko wenarha
 namagugu wenarha le njengezinto eziqakathekileko ukunothisa amagugu amumethwe emThethweni-
 sisekelo ; begodu

 • Ukuthembeka, ikhwalithi nekghono : ukunikela ifundo emadanisekako ngekhwalithi, ububanzi
 nangokudepha kwefundo yakezinye iinarha.

(e) IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu yeliZweloke amaGreyidi R-12 sinqophe ukukhiqiza abafundi abakwazi uku:

 • bona nokurarulula imiraro nokuthatha iinqunto kusetjenziswa ukucabanga ngelihlo elibukhali
 nelokuzitlamela ;

 • sebenzisana ngepumelelo nabanye njengamalunga wesiqhema ;

 • hlela nokuziphatha begodu nokuphatha imisebenzi yabo ngokuziphendulela nangepumelelo ;

 • buthelela, ukutsenga, ukuhlela bese bahlaziya ilwazi ngelihlo lokuhlaba ;

 • khulumisana ngepumelelo basebenzisa amakghono wokubonwako/wokubukelwako, wamatshwayo
 begodu/nanyana welimi ngeendlela ezinengi sebenzisa isayensi nethekhnoloji ngepumelelo batjengise,
 ngelihlo lokuhlaba, nokuziphendulela manqophana nebhodulukweni neempilo zabanye; noku

 • Tjengisa ukuzwisisa iphasi bonyana linamahlelo anetjhebiswano ngokuyelela bonyana ukurarulula imiraro
 akwenzeki kukodwa ngeqadi.

(f) Ukuhlanganisa imihlobo ehlukahlukeneko yabafundi kufanele kube mgogodlha womkhandlu, wokuhlela
 begodu nokufundisa kileso naleso sikolo. Lokhu kungenzeka kwaphela nange boke abotitjhere banokuzwisisa
 okuhluzekileko bokobana iinqabo zokufunda zingalemukwa bezilungiswe bunjani, begodu ukuhlanganisa
 kungahlelelwa bunjani

 Okuqakathekileko ngokuhlanganisa imihlobo ehlukahlukeneko yabafundi kuqinisekisa bonyana iinqabo
 ziyabonwa bezitjhejwa ngiyo yoke imikhandlu efaneleko ehlangana nomphakathi wesikolo, okufaka hlangana
 abotitjhere, iinQhema eziSekelako eziNzinze kumaDistrigi, iinQhema eziSekelako eziseZingeni leenHlangano,
 ababelethi neenKolo eziKhethekileko njengeendawo zemiThombo. Ukulungisa iinqabo ngetlasini, abotitjhere
 bafanele basebenzise amaqhinga amanengi wekharikhyulamu afana nalawo afakwe ku- Guidelines for
 Inclusive Teaching and Learning (2010) yoMnyango weFundo Sisekelo

ISIGABA 2 UKWETHULA AMALIMI

2.1 AMALIMI ESITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA

Ilimi lisisetjenziswa/sitlabagelo somcabango nokuthintana. Libuye libe yindlela yesiko kanye neyokuzikhakhazisa
kanengi abantu ababelana ngalo ukwenza bona iphasi abahlala kilo lizwakale ngcono. Ukufunda ukusebenzisa
ilimi ngendlela efaneleko kwenza abafundi bathole ilwazi, bazitjho imvelaphi yabo, imizwa kanye nemibono,
basebenzisane nabanye, begodu balawule iphasi labo. Linikela godu abafundi ihlelo leenthombe ezinothileko,
ezinamandla nezinemvelaphi enengi kanye nemibono engasetjenziswa ukwenza iphasi labo libe ngcono kunalokho
elingikho; likhazimule kunalokho elingikho. Kungesimanga selimi ukobana ukungafani ngokwamasiko kanye
nokuhlobana ngokwehlalakuhle kutjengiswe bekubunjwe, begodu kungesimanga selimi ukobana izakhiwo ezinjalo
zingatjhugululwa, zinatjiswe begodu zenziwe ngcono.

2.1.1 Amazinga welimi

Ukufunda ilimi esiGabeni eliPhakathi kufaka hlangana woke amalimi asemthethweni eSewula Afrika, okuyi-Afrikaans,
English, isiNdebele, isiXhosa, isiZulu, iSepedi (iSesotho sase Leboa), iSesotho, iSetswana, iSiswati, iTshivenda,
iXitsonga – begodu namaLimi angasisemThethweni. Amalimi la angafundiswa emazingeni ahlukahlukeneko welimi.

ILimi leKhaya lilimi elifundwa qangi bafundi. Nanyana kunjalo, inengi leenkolo zeSewula Afrika aziwafundisi amanye
amalimi weKhaya wabanye abafundi nofana wabo boke abafundi abatlolisileko kodwana zinelimi linye nofana mabili
afundiswa ezingeni leLimi leKhaya. Ngonobangela walokho, amalebula afana neLimi leKhaya, iLimi lokuThoma
lokwEngeza atjho amazinga wobuhlakani ilimi elifundiswa kiwo ingasi indabuko(ilimi leKhaya) nofana elitholiweko
(njengemalimini wokwEngeza.) Mayelana neminqopho yomthetho-kambiso lo, okhunye nokhunye okutjhiwo
kuqaliswe eLimini leKhaya kufanele kuzwisiseke bonyana kutjho izinga ingasi ilimi ngokwalo.

Izinga leLimi leKhaya linikela ubuhlakani belimi obutjengisa isisekelo samakghono wokuthintana wobuyena
bakhe atlhogekako ebujameni bokuhlalisana begodu nemakghonweni wefundo aqakathekileko wokufunda kiyo
yoke ikharikhyulamu. Kugandelelwa bonyana kufundiswe amakghono wokulalela, ukukhuluma, ukufunda begodu
newokutlola kilelizinga lelimi. Izinga leli linikela godu abafundi amakghono wezemitlolo, ukukarekela umtlolo
nokubona izinto ngamehlo wengqondo okuzabanikela ikghono lokwakha ngobutjha, ukufanekisa/ukubona ngamehlo
wengqondo, nokwenza ngcono ukuzwisisa iphasi abahlala kilo. Nanyana kunjalo, ukugandelela kanye nesilinganiso
sesikhathi sokulalela nokukhuluma ukusuka eGreyidini ye- 7 ukuya phezulu kungaphasi kunawo amakghono
wokufunda nokutlola.

Ilimi lokuThoma lokwEngeza litjho ilimi elingasilo ilimi lekhaya kodwana lisetjenziswa eminqopheni ethileko
yokuthintana emphakathini, okutjho ukuthi, lilimi lokufunda nokufundisa efundweni.Ikharikhyulamu inikela isisekelo
esinamandla ebafundini labo abazokusebenzisa ilimi lokuthoma lokwEngeza njengelimi lokufunda nokufundisa
efundweni. Ekupheleni kweGreyidi ye-9 abafundi kufanele bakwazi ukusebenzisa ilimi lekhaya begodu nelimi
lokuthoma lokwEngeza ngefanelo begodu nangokuzithemba eminqopheni ehlukahlukeneko, efaka hlangana
ukufunda.

ESewula Afrika, inengi labafundi lithoma ukusebenzisa ilimi lokwEngeza, i-English, njengelimi lokufunda nokufundisa
eGreyidini ye-4. Lokhu kutjho bona kufanele bafinyelele ezingeni eliphezulu lekghono lesiNgisi ekupheleni kweGreyidi
yesi-3.

Izinga leLimi lokuThoma lokwEngeza lithatha sengathi abafundi abamane babe nelinye nelinye ilwazi lelimi lokha
nabafika esikolweni. Umqopho eminyakeni embalwa yokuthoma yesikolweni kukuthuthukisa amakghono wabafundi

 8 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
wokuzwisisa nokukhuluma ilimi – amakghono asisekelo wokuthintana ngokwakhe. EGreyidini yesi- 2 neye- 3 abafundi
bathoma ukwakha ikghono lokutlola nokufunda kilesisisekelo sezomlomo. Basebenzisa namakghono welitheresi
esele bawafundile ngeLimi labo leKhaya.

EsiGabeni esiPhakathi nesiGabeni esiPhakamileko, abafundi baragela phambili nokuqinisa amakghono wabo
wokulalela, ukukhuluma, ukufunda begodu nokutlola. Kilesi isigaba inengi labafundi bafunda ngeLimi lokuThoma
lokwEngeza, okuyi-English, begodu bafanele bathole ukuvezelwa kilo khulu. Ukugandelela okukhulu kubekwa
eLimini lokuThoma lokwEngeza ngomqopho wokucabanga nokucabangisisa. Lokhu kwenza abafundi bathuthukise
amakghono wabo aphathelene nefundo, abawadingako ukuze bafunde iimfundo ezinjengeSayensi esiNgisini.
Bazozibandakanya khulu namatheksthi wezemitlolo begodu bathome ukuthuthukisa amakghono wobuthandabuza
newokucabangisisa elimini lokwEngeza.

Lokha abafundi nasele bangena esiGabeni eliPhakamileko, kufanele bonyana babe nobuhlakani obubonakalako
eLimini labo lokuThoma lokwEngeza malungana nekghono letjhebiswano ngokwakhe nekghono eliphathelene
nengqondo nokufunda. Nanyana kunjalo, iqiniso kukobana inengi labafundi alikghoni ukukhulumisana nanyana
ukuthintana kuhle ngeLimi lokwEngeza kilesisigaba. Ngakho ke, isitjhijilo esiGabeni esiPhakathi, kunikela isekelo
kilabo bafundi ngesikhathi esifanako unikela ikharikhyulamu eyenza abafundi bakghone ukuhlangabezana
namazinga afunekako emagreyidini asezako. Amazinga lawo kufanele abe ngendlela yokobana abafundi bakghone
ukusebenzisa iLimi labo lokwEngeza ezingeni eliphakameko lobuhlakani ukubalungiselela ifundo esezako nofana
ephakamileko nofana iphasi lomsebenzi.

Kungonobangela loyo ukobana izinga lengqondo leLimi lokuThoma lokwEngeza kufanele libe ngemdlela yokobana
lisetjenziswe njengelimi lokufunda nokufundisa. Amakghono wokulalela, ukukhuluma nokusetjenziswa kwelimi
azokuthuthukiswa ukuya phambili begodu nokwenziwa ngcono esiGabeni esiPhakathi ekuthuthukiseni amakghono
wabafundi wokufunda nokutlola.

2.1.2 Amakghono welimi

Ikharikhyulamu yeLimi lokuThoma lokwEngeza ihlelwe ngokuya ngamakghono alandelako:

 1 Ukulalela nokukhuluma
 2 Ukufunda nokubukela
 3 Ukutlola nokwethula
 4 Isakhiwo nemithetho yelimi

Ukulalela nokukhuluma

Ukulalela nokukhuluma kumgogodlha oqakathekileko ekufundeni zoke iimfundo. Ngokwamaqhinga afaneleko
wokuLalela nokuKhuluma, abafundi babuthelela begodu bahlanganise ilwazi, bebakhe ilwazi, bararulule imiraro,
begodu baveze imibono begodu nemicabango. Amakghono wokulalela ngelihlo elihlabako enza abafundi bakghone
ukubona amagugu begodu nemikghwa engaphakathi kwamatheksthi begodu baphose iselele elimini elithatha
ubuhlangothi neliphatha buqobolwana.

EsiGabeni esiPhakathi, abafundi beLimi lokuThoma lokwEngeza bazokusebenzisa amakghono wokuLalela
nokuKhuluma ukukhulumisana nokubonisana ngehlathululo. Bazokwakhela phezu kwamakghono athuthukiswe
esiGabeni esisiSekelo ukudlulisa iinkulumiswano, iingcoco, nokwethula iinkulumo ezifitjhani zomlomo ngokuragako.

EsiGabeni lesi, ilimi lomfundi alikhulumako lisadinga ukutjengiswa nokusekelwa, isibonelo, ngelwazimagama
namafremu wemitjho). Utitjhere kufanele aqinisekise bonyana boke abafundi bafunyana amathuba wokukhuluma

TTKH 9
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
ngesiNdebele. Ngombana abantwana bazokuragela phambili ngebelo elihlukahlukeneko, abotitjhera badinga
bonyana batjhugulule amathuba wokukhuluma (isib. Imibuzo ayibuzako) ilungele izinga lomfundi ngamunye.
Njengombana bathuthuka ngokuya ngamagreyidi, abotitjhere bafanele balindele bonyana abafundi bakhuluma khulu
begodu nokukhuluma kwabo kuzokuba kudanyana.

Ukufunda kuzokwakhela emihlotjeni yamatheksthi ethulwe esiGabeni esisiSekelo (isib. iindatjana, ukubika/ukucoca
ngaye, begodu neenlayelo) begodu abafundi bazokwethulelwa imihlobo emitjha yamatheksthi (isib.ukubika/ukucoca
ngokulamana kwamaphuzu, iindatjana ezihlukahlukeneko zemitlolo, imibiko yomlomo, iinkulumo ezifitjhani).

Isikhathi sokufundisa sifanele sifake hlangana ukuzijayeza kwangamalanga kwemisebenzi emifitjhani yokuLalela
nokuKhuluma kanye nemisebenzi emide enqophileko esatjalaliswa ngeveke.

Ukufunda nokubukela

Amakghono wokuFunda nokuBukela athuthukiswe kuhle amgogodlha wokufunda ngepumelelo kiyo yoke
ikharikhyulamu. Abafundi bayathuthuka ngekghono lokufunda nokubukela kuhle amatheksthi ahlukahlukeneko
wezemitlolo begodu nangasiyo yezemitlolo, uku faka hlangana amatheksthi abukelwako. Abafundi balemuka indlela
zemitlolo kanye nerejista zitjengisa ngakho umnqopho, abamukelilwazi begodu nobujamo bamatheksthi. Ngokufunda
etlasini begodu nangokuzijamela, abafundi baba babantu abacabanga ngelihlo lokuhlaba begodu nangobuhlakani.

EsiGabeni esiPhakathi, uzokwakhela esisekelweni esindlalwe eGreyidini R -3. Nakudingekako, sebenzisa ukufunda
ngokwabelana ekuthomeni kweGreyidi yesi-4 ukukhokhela abafundi kilesi isigaba. Ungasebenzisa indlela le kesinye
isikhathi nangabe uneenCwadi eziKulu ezaneleko kileli izinga, bese ujamiselela ngokucoca indatjana. Nawunganazo
iinCwadi eziKulu kilelizinga, sebenzisa amatheksthi aseencwadini zokufundisa nofana eencwadini zabafundi
zokufunda. Ungasebenzisa iindlela efana nokuFunda nabafundi begodu nokuFundela itlasi loke.

Sebenzisa iindlela zokufunda kwesiqhema esikhokhelwako begodu nangokuzijamela/ngababili begodu kancani
kancani wenza abafundi bafunde kanengi ngokuzijamela.Ukufunda ngokuzijamela okutlolwe emahlelweni
wokufundisa kufanele angeniswe esikhathini esabelwe ukufundisa. Khuthaza abafundi bakho bonyana bafunde
ngokuzijamela kesinye nesinye isikhathi sokuledlha abanaso.

Uzokwenza imisebenzi yokuzwisisa ehlukahlukeneko ukuqinisekisa bonyana abafundi bayakuzwisisa abakufundako.

Indlela yekambiso yokufunda

Indlela yekambiso yokufunda ineengaba ezilandelako: Ukulungiselela ukufunda, ukufunda, begodu nangemva
kokufunda. Imisebenzi ezokwenziwa mfundi ingarhunyezwa ngendlela elandelako:

Ukulungiselela ukufunda:

• Ukuhlahlambisa ilwazi asele analo

• Ukuqala umthombo, umtloli, begodu nedadamu/ilanga eveza ukukhutjhwa kwencwadi leyo

• Ukufunda isigaba/ipharagrafu yokuthoma neyokugcina.

• Ukwenza ibonelo phambili

 10 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
Ukufunda:

• Ukunande uphumula uhlole ukuzwisisa okufundako nokwenza imibono ingeneleleko

• Ukumadanisa okumunyethweko nebonelo phambili lakho

• Ukusebenzisa okumunyethweko ukufunyana ihlathululo yamagama angaziwako nakukghonekako,
 nakungakghonekiko, sebenzisa isihlathululi-mezwi.

• Ukubona ngelihlo lengqondo lokho akufundako

• Ukuragela phambili nokufunda nanyana kuneengcenyana angazizwisisiko.

• Ukubuyelela nanyana angazwisisi.Ukufundela phezulu iingaba eziraranisako, kabuthaka.

• Ukubawa isizo nakangazwisisiko

• Ukusebenzisa amatshwayo wokufunda nokwenza amanothi ngamaphuzu aqakathekileko

• Ukuzindla ngalokho okufundileko

Ngemva kokufunda:

• Nawuzokudinga ukukhumbula ilwazi elinqophileko, gwala umfanekiso wokuhlela nofana ukwendlala imibono
 eqakathekileko begodu nemininingwana esekelalo embalwa

• Ukuthatha iinqunto

• Ukutlola ukurhunyeza okuzokusiza ukutlhadlhula nokukhumbula imibono eqakathekileko

• Ukucabanga nokutlola imibuzo emitjha onayo ngesihloko

• Ukuzibuza bonyana umnqopho uwufezile/uwufinyelele na?

• Ukuzwisisa- ukuqinisekisa ukuzwisisa kwakho itheksthi

• Ukuhlaziya–ubuhlangothi, ukunemba, nekhwalithi/ubunjalo yetheksthi

• Ukunabisa umcabango wakho- ukusebenzisa imibono oyibone etheksthini

UkuTlola nokwEthula

UkuTlola kusisetjenziswa/kusitlabagelo esinamandla sokuthintana esivumela abafundi bona bakhe bebaveze
imibono ethungeleleneko. Ukuzijayeza ukunande utlola amatheksthi amanengi ahlukahlukeneko, imisebenzi
begodu nezinye iimfundo kwenza abafundi bakghone ukuthintana okusebenzako nangobuhlakani. Ukutlola
okuthuthuka ngamazinga afaneleko ngokusebenzisa amafremu, kukhupha abatloli abanekghono, nelwazi elijulileko
abazokukghona ukusebenzisa amakghono wabo ukuthuthukisa nokwethula amatheksthi atloliweko, abukelwako
neenrhatjhi-nengi ahlukahlukeneko ngokweminqopho ehlukahlukeneko. EsiGabeni esiPhakathi, abafundi beLimi
lokuThoma lokwEngeza bazokudinga itjhejo lokusekelwa nokuhlahlwa ukuthuthukisa amakghono wokukhupha
amatheksthi anzinzileko.

Ukutlola kuqakathekile ngombana kukatelelela abafundi bona bacabange ngehlelo lelimi nangokupeleda. Lokhu
kukhuthaza abafundi bonyana bathuthukise ilimi, kuthuthukiswe ukuzuzwa kwelimi begodu nokungeza ukunemba.

TTKH 11
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
Abafundi bazakufunda ukutlola amatheksthi wokuzitlamela newokwazisa ahlukahlukeneko, bathome ngokusebenzisa
amafremu njengesekelo begodu kancani kancani bafunde ukutlola imihlobo yamatheksthi athileko ngokuzijamela.
Bazakusebenzisa godu indlela yekambiso yokutlola ukukhiqiza/ukukhupha amatheksthi atlolwako ahleleke kuhle,
anehlelo elinembako.

Indlela yekambiso yokutlola

Ukutlola nokutlama amatheksthi kuyikambiso emumethe amagadango alandelako:

• Ukulungiselela ukutlola/ukuhlela

• Ukutlhatlhabeja/Umtlamo wokuthoma

• Ukubuyekeza

• Uku-editha/ukulungisa iimphoso

• Ukukhupha/ukwethula umtlamo yokugcina

Kufanele abafundi bazibandule kilekambiso ngoku:

• qunta ngomnqopho nabamukeli lwazi betheksthi etlolwako begodu/nofana etlanywako;

• lahlela/ukuphosela imibono ngokusebenzisa, isibonelo amamebhengqondo, amaflowu- tjhadi namarhelo;

• funda imithombo efaneleko, bakhetha ilwazi elinembako begodu bahlele imibono;

• khupha umtlamo wokuthoma oyelele umnqopho, abamukeli lwazi, isihloko nesakhiwo setheksthi;

• funda imitlhatlhabejo ngelihlo lokuhlaba/elibukhali begodu bafumane nombiko obuya kwabanye (abafundi
 abafunda ngetlasini yinye nomfundi nofana utitjhere)

• editha nokulungisa iimphoso zomtlhatlhabejo

• khupha umtlamo wokugcina ohlwengekileko, obonakalako, onganaamphoso.

IZakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yeLimi

Ilwazi elihle lelwazi-magama nehlelo lelimi elifundwe kumaGreyidi R - 3, linikela isisekelo sokuthuthukisa amakghono
(wokulalela, ukukhuluma, ukufunda nokutlola) eLimini lokuThoma lokweNgeza. Abafundi besiGabeni esiPhakathi
bazokwakhela phezu kwesisekelo esindlalwe egreyidini R-3.

Abafundi bazakufunda bona iZakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yokusetjenziswa kweLimi zisetjenziswa bunjani,
bazokuthuthukisa ilimi lokwabelana lokukhuluma ngelimi (‘imeta- language”), ukuze bakwazi ukuhlaziya amatheksthi
wabo namanye ngelihlo elibukhali ngokwehlathululo, ngefanelo nokunemba. Bazokukghona ukusebenzisa
ilwazi leli ukwenza umsebenzi wokulinga ngelimi ukwakha ihlathululo izinga legama nomutjho ukuya kuwo woke
amatheksthi, begodu nokubona bonyana itheksthi nobujamo kuhlobana bunjani. Ngokuzibandakanya namatheksthi
ahlukahlukeneko, abafundi bangeza uusebenzisa kwabo Ilwazimagama begodu basebenzisa ilwazi labo leZakhiwo
nemiThetjhwana yokusetjenziswa kweLimi.

Ngokuzibandakanya nemihlobo ehlukahlukeneko yamatheksthi, abafundi bangeza ukusebenzisa kwabo
kwelwazimagama begodu basebenzise ilwazi labo leZakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yeLimi.. EsiGabeni esiPhakathi,

 12 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
abafundi beLimi lokuThoma lokwEngeza bazokutjheja khulu ukwakhiwa kwamagama nehlelo esele balazi elitholakele
esiGabeni esisiSekelo, ukuhlola indlela ilimi labo lokwEngeza lisebenza ngayo begodu baliyelele, balisebenze ilwazi
elithuthukakoukuhlola ukusetjenziswa kwelimi, khulukhulu nabatlolako.

Abafundi bazokuhlola kobana ilimi lisetjenziswa bunjani, begodu bazokuthuthukisa ilimi lokwabelana
lokukhuluma ngelimi (“imeta- language”), ukuze bakwazi ukuhlaziya amatheksthi wabo namanye ngelihlo elibukhali
ngokwehlathululo, ngefanelo nokunemba. Bazokukghona ukusebenzisa ilwazi leli ukwenza umsebenzi wokulinga
ngelimi ukwakha ihlathululo (ukusuka kuzinga legamanomutjho ukuya kiwo woke amatheksthi), begodu nokubona
bonyana itheksthi nobujamo babo buhlobana bunjani.

Kulindeleke bonyana iZakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yeLimi zifundiswe ebujameni njengombana amanye amakghono
welimi afundiswa begodu athuthukiswa. Amahlelo wokufundisa amumethe amarhelo wezinto ezizokufundiswa
egreyidini ngayinye.Nawukhetha amatheksthi wokulalela nokufunda womzombe weemveke ezimbili qinisekisa
bonyana kubakhona ezinye izinto eziphathelene zelimi ofuna ukuzifundisa. Tlama imisebenzi ehlobene namatheksthi
lawa azokwenza abafundi basebenzise izinto lezi, ebujameni obuthileko. Ngokufanako, amatheksthi azokutlolwa
bafundi azokufaka hlangana ezinye izinto ezitholakala elimini.Khokhela abafundi bakho bonyana basebenzise izinto
eziphathelene nokusetjenziswa ngefanelo kwelimi. Khetha ezinye izinto eziphathelene nelimi abafundi abanobudisi
kizo begodu ubanikele imisetjenzana yokuzijayeza ehlelekileko. EsiGabeni esiPhakathi, amamiithi amatjhumi
amathathu ngeveke abekelwe ukufunda okuhlelekileko eZakhiweni nemThetjhwaneni yeLimi.

2.1.3 Iindlela zokufundisa ilimi

Iindlela zokufundisa ilimi zidzimelele ematheksthini, ekuthintaneni begodu neekambisweni. Iindlela edzimelele
ematheksthi nekuthintaneni zombili zisime ekurageleni phambili kokusetjenziswa nokukhiqizwa kwamatheksthi.

Indlela edzimelele ematheksthini ihlola indlela yokusebenza kwamatheksthi. Umnqopho wekambiso kusiza abafundi
bona babenekghono, bazithembe, babe bafundi abanelihlo lokuhlaba, abatloli nababukeli bamatheksthi. Indlela
le ifaka hlangana ukulalela, ukufunda, ukubukela nokutsenga amatheksthi khona bazokuzwisisa bona akhiqizwe
bunjani begodu anamiphi imithelela. Ngokuthintana ngelihlo elihlabako abafundi bathuthukisa ikghono lokuhlaziya
amatheksthi. Indlela edzimelele ematheksthini godu ibandakanya ukukhiqiza amatheksthi ahlukahlukeneko
ngomnqopho nabamukeli-lwazi abathileko. Indlela le idzimelele ekuzwisiseni bona amatheksthi abunjwa bunjani.
Indlela le izakufuna ukufanekisa okunengi, ukusekela begodu nokunikela umtlhala ngetlasini eLimini lokuThoma
lokwEngeza. Iimphakamiso zalokhu zakhele emahlelweni wokufundisa.

Indlela edzimelele ekuthintaneni iphakamisa bonyana lokha nakufundwa ilimi, umfundi kufanele abe mumuntu
okhulela kilo begodu abe namathuba amanengi wokuzijayeza lona nofana wokulikhiqiza ngokukhulumisana/
ngokuthintana ngeminqopho yokuhlalisana nofana ukuzijayeza. Ukufunda ilimi kufanele kube ngokwemvelo, indlela
yekambiso engakahleleki idluliselwe ngetlasini lapho amakghono welitheresi wokufunda/nokubukela begodu
wokutlola/nokwethula afundwa ngendlela ‘yemvelo’-Abafundi bafunda ngokufunda khulu begodu bafunda ukutlola
imihlobo eminengi yemitlolo.

TTKH 13
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
2.2 UKWABIWA KWESIKHATHI SELIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA EKHARIKHYULAMINI

Isikhathi sokufundisa iLimi lokuThoma lokwEngeza esiGabeni esiPhakathi sima-iri ama-5 ngeveke. Koke
okumunyethwe lilimi kunikelwe ama-iri ali-10 ngomzombe weemveke ezimbili (ama-iri ali-10). Ukwabiwa kwesikhathi
okulandelako kwamakghono ahlukahlukeneko welimi aphakanyisiwe:

 Amakghono Ukwabiwa kwesikhathi ngokuya ngomzombe weemveke ezimbili (ama-iri)
 Igreyidi 4 Igreyidi 5 Igreyidi 6

 14 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
Utitjhere kufanele abe nalokhu:

a) IsiTatimende seKharikhyulamu nomThetho-kambiso nokuHlola

b) UmThetho-kambiso weLimi kwezeFundo (i-LiEP)

c) Incwadi etlolelwe ukufundisa isifundo esithileko eqintelwe iLimi lokuThoma lokweNgeza esetjenziswa bafundi;
 nezinye iincwadi zokufunda ezimithombo ukungezelela keziqintelweko.

d) Incwadi yokufunda emumethe imihlobo yamatheksthi ephakanyisiweko

e) Iinhlathululimezwi begodu neencwadi ezimithombo (ezilimi-linye, limi-mbili, begodu nezilimi-nengi; ithesarasi,
 i-encyclopaedi, iincwadi ezimithombo zehlelo

f) Ifayili/inCwadi kaTitjhere emthombo: lokhu kungaba yifayili enamametheriyela abuthelelwe nguthitjhera nofana
 umhlahlandlela kaTitjhera ogadangiselwe ukuthengisa.

Iinsiza zangetlasini

a) Amathektsi wokufunda ngokwabelana weGreyidi yesi-4. Kungaba ‘ziinCwadi eziKulu’ nofana amanye
 amatheksthi akhulisiweko nofana inamatheksthi akhulisiweko nofana iincwadi zokufunda eziqintelweko.

b) Imihlobohlobo yamathektsi eqalelela amazinga ahlukeneko wokufunda, isib. ibuthelelo leencwadi zokufunda
 ezinamatheksthi alingene walelo nalelo zinga letlasi/lesiqhema.Incwadi eyodwayokufunda ingasetjenziselwa
 ukufunda ngababili/ngokuzijamela

c) Imihlobo ehlukahlukeneko yamametheriyali aziinrhatjhi: amaphephandaba, amamagazini, amabrowutjha,
 amaflaya, iinkhangiso, amaphostara, izaziso, njll.

d) Iinsiza ezizwakalako/ezibonakalako

TTKH 19
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
 Izakhiwo nemithetjhwana yelimi
Okumumethweko okufanele kwenziwe kurhenyiswe ngenzasi. Amakghono namakghonyana: Abafundi bafanele babone,
bazwisise begodu basebenzise izakhiwo nemithetjhwana yehlelo erhenyiswe ngenzasi ebujameni obuthileko. Bafanele
bazwisise bonyana imithetho isetjenziswa bunjani. Ukuthuthukiswa kwelwazimagama kufakiwe esigabeni lesi.
 AMABIZO
Ukubuyekeza amabizo afundwe esiGabeni esisiSekelo.
Ukubuyekeza amabizovama: amabizo abalekako isib. incwadi – iincwadi namabizo angabalekiko isib. Itjhukela, iinhluthu.
Ukusebenzisa amabizo anobunengi kwaphela isib. amanzi, iinzibi
Ukubuyekeza ubumnini bebizo isib. Ipumulo kaBongi
Ukusebenzisa amabizo muntu ngefanelo isib. ngamagabhadhlela.
Ukusebenzisa ubulili bamanye amabizo isib. Ikomo - ikunzi
Ukusebenzisa amabizo wezinto ongekhe wazibona ngamehlo wenyama isib. umoya, ithando.
Ukusebenzisa amabizo wezinto eziphathekako isib. isitulo, ideski.
Ukusebenzisa amabizo – mvango isib. Ntombizodwa, Velabahlinze
Ukusebenzisa iinthomo neenlungelelo zamabizo ngefanelo
Ukusebenzisa iinciphiso neenkhuliso ngefanelo
IINQOPHISI/AMADITHEMENA
Ukusebenzisa iinqophisi ezitjengisa ubungako/ubunengi, isib. kunye, kubili begodu (nelandelano) kokuthoma,
kwesibili, kwamaswaphela, okunye, mbadlwana, okunengi, ngokwaneleko
 IZABIZWANA
Ukubuyekeza ukusetjenziswa kwesabizwana samambala isib. Mina, wena, yena, kona, bona, wena, yena, kona, thina.
Ukubuyekeza ukusetjenziswa kwesabizwana sokukhomba isib. Le, leyo, lezo, lezi.
Ukusebenzisa isabizwana sobumnini isib. Yami, yakho, yakhe, kwethu, zabo.
Isakhi sokuzenza, isib. Ngiyazithanda
 IIMPHAWULO
Ukubuyekeza ukusebenzisa isiphawulo ngaphambi nangemva kwebizo isib. Encani inja,
Ukusebenzisa isiphawulo ngemva kwebizo isib. Inja encani
Ukusebenzisa imihlobo ehlukahlukileko yeemphawulo okufaka hlangana lezo eziphathelene neminyaka isib. Omdala,
omutjha; iziqu zesiphawulo ngilezi:, -mbi, -de, -hle, -dala, -fitjhani, -ncani, khulu, -bili, -thathu, njll
 IZENZO
Ukuthuthukisa ukusetjenziswa kwesivumelwano sehloko emtjhweni, isib. Ubaba ukapa iinkuni.
Ukusebenzisa indlela yesenzo ekatelelako, isib.Jama! Khamba!
Ukusebenzisa isenzo sibe sesikhathini sanje, nesidlulileko, isib. Khamba, khambile, wakhamba
Ukusebenzisa iinsizasenzo ‘ba’
Ukusebenzisa isakhi sokulandula esenzweni, isib. Ukungalali/ukungakhulumi
Imihlobo yezenzo

 20 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6

 IINKATHI ZESENZO
Ukuthuthukisa iinkhathi zesenzo ezisetjenziswe esiGabeni esisiSekelo ukufaka hlangana isikhathi esidlulileko, sanje,
nesikhathi sanje esiragela phambili, nesizako
Ukusebenzisa iinkhathi ngefanelo emihlotjeni ehlukahlukeneko yamatheksthi afundiweko, ukufaka hlangana:
• Isikhathi sanje esihlathulula izenzeko ezivamileko, isib. “Ngihlamba amazinyo wami ngamalanga begodu neentatimende
 mazombe, isib.” Ilanga litjhinga eTjhingalanga”
Ukusebenzisa iinkhathi ngefanelo emihlotjeni ehlukahlukeneko yamatheksthi efundiweko, ukufaka hlangana:
• Isikhathi sanje esipheleleko, isib. Ngihlezi eThekwini ubuphilo bami boke.
Ukusebenzisa iinkhathi ngefanelo emihlotjeni ehlukahlukeneko yamatheksthi efundiweko, ukufaka hlangana:
• Isikhathi esidlulileko esiragako sesenzeko ebesisaragela phambili lokha esinye nasenzekako, isib. Belisina lokha nasisuka
 ekhaya .
Ukusebenzisa iinkhathi ngefanelo emihlotjeni ehlukahlukeneko yamatheksthi efundiweko, ukufaka hlangana:
• Isikhathi esizako ngokusebenzisa isikhathi sanje esiragela phambili, isib. Siya eKapa ngeveke ezako .

 IINSIZASENZO
Ukubuyekeza ukusetjenziswa kweensizasenzo, isib. “nga” ukutjengisa ikghono, “Nga “ ukutjengisa ukubawa iimvumo
Ukuthoma ukusebenzisa “kufanele” ukutjengisa ukukatelela nefuneko
Ukusebenzisa “za-“/”ya-“ ukutjengisa isifiso
Ukusebenzisa “za-“ ukutjengisa into esazokwenzeka, isib. Kuzokuba neenwuruwuru namhlanje.
Ukusebenzisa u-“ngahle” ukudlulisa ikghonakalo.
 IINABISO/IZANDISO
Ukuthoma ukusebenzisa iinabizo zesikhathi, isib. Kusasa, izolo ;iinabiso zendawo,
Ukuthoma ukusebenzisa iinabizo zendawo isib. Lapha, ekhaya, esitolo
Ukuthoma ukusebenzisa iinabizo zesimo, isib. Msinyana, kabuthaka
 IINHLANGANISO
Ukusebenzisa iinhlanganiso ukutjengisa ukuhlanganisa isib. Begodu; ukulamanisa isib. bese; okulandelako
Ukusebenzisa iinhlanganiso ukutjengisa ukuhlukanisa, isib. kodwana
Ukusebenzisa iinhlanganiso ukutjengisa isizathu, isib. ngombana
 UKWAKHIWA KWEMITJHO
Ukwakha imitjho elula
Ukwakha imitjho epandepande
Ukusebenzisa indlela ekatelelako
Ukusebenzisa indlela yokubuza, isib. Ngubani, yini, nini, yiphi, kuba yini, bunjani
Ukusebenzisa indlela elandulako
Ukusebenzisa umbandela, isib. Nangabe...... bese....
Ukusebenzisa ipambosi yokwenziwa.
Ukusebenzisa ikulumo enqophileko isib. Umma uyabuza, “ Ngiwatjhiye kuphi amakhiya wami?”
Ukuthoma ukukhumbula nokusebenzisa ikulumo ebikwako, isib. Umma uyabuza, “ Ngiwatjhiye kuphi amakhiya wami?” ;
Umma uyabuza bonyana uwatjhiye kuphi amakhiya wakhe.

TTKH 21
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
 AMATSHWAYO WOKUTLOLA NOKUFUNDA
Ukubuyekeza amatshwayo wokutlola nokufunda afundwe esigabeni esisisSekelo, isib. Amagabhadlhela, abongqi,
amakhoma, abonobuza.
Ukusebenzisa amagabhadlhela kumabizomuntu, eenhlokweni begodu neenrhunyezweni zamagama wabantu
Ukusebenzisa amakhoma ukuhlukanisa amabizo aserhelweni
Ukusebenzisa iimbabazo
Ukusebenzisa i-aphostrofi nawukhuluma burhaba, isib. Ngifun’ imali.
Ukusebenzisa abodzubhula ekulumeni enqophileko
Ukusebenzisa ikholoni ukutjengisa ikulumo enqophileko emtlolweni womdlalo wesiteji nofana kukulumo-pendulwano;
ukusebenzisa ikholoni ukurhemisa izinto.
 UKUTHUTHUKISA ILWAZIMAGAMA
Ukuthoma ukuzwisisa nokusebenzisa:
-- Amagama awela ngesibayeni esisodwa, isib. Ukutsu nenja “ ziimbandana zekhaya.”
-- Amagama amqondofana (amagama atjho izinto/iinhlathululo ezifanako, isib. msinyana/burhaba)
-- Amagama amqondophika (amagama anehlathululo ehlukileko), isib.phezulu/phasi)
-- Ibizwafana (amagama elitlolwa beliphinyiswe ngokufanako, nokho kube aneenhlathululo ezahlukeneko, isib. Ithanga, izulu
Ukukhumbula bonyana amagama akheka bunjani
• Amagama mvango, isib. Ntombizodwa, Mzwakhe, Khethukuthula, Siphosezwe
• Ukufaka isithomo namkha isilungelelo esiqwini segama
• Ukurhunyeza amagama, isib. uTjhirhweni/Tjh, uRhoboyi/Rhob.
• Iinrhunyezo, isib. Njalonjalo/njll; amakhronimI, isib. SADTU, SOWETO
Ukuthoma ukuzwisisa nokusebenzisa izimo zekulumo Begins to understand and use some fixed expressions
• Izenzo ezimimitjho, isib. Khwela uye phezulu, yehlela phasi, ngena ngaphakathi
• Ibuthelelo, isib. Langa elihle lamabeletho, ifesi namatjhipsi
• Izaga, isib. Ligotjwa lisemanzi
• Izitjho, isib. Ukuba nesandla.
 UKUPELEDA NEMITHETHO YOKUPELEDA
Ukwakhela phezu kwelwazi lokuphinyiswa kwamagama afundwe esiGabeni esisiSekelo, isib.Ukwakha imindeni yamagama
ngokuya ngokuthi aphinyiswa bunjani nofana aqaleka bunjani.
Ukwakhelphezu kwelwazi lamagama afundwa ngokubona begodu namagama avela kaneng emtlolweni afundwe esiGabeni
esisiSekelo.
Ukupeleda amagama ajayelekileko ngefanelo, ngokusebenzisa isihlathululo-mezwi sakhe, okufaka hlangana amagama
avamise ukuraranisa, isib.raga, rarha
Ukusebenzisa isihlathululi-mezwi ukuhlola ukupeleda nehlathululo yamagama.
Ukubuyelela afunde godu umtlolo wakhe begodu enze iinlungiso zokupeleda.
Ukusebenzisa ilwazi lokulandelanisa amaledere wama-alfabhethi begodu namaledere wokuthoma wegama ukuthola
amagama kusihlathululi-mezwi.
Ukwakha amagama amatjha ngokutjhugulula iledere linye/itjhada elijamiselela ngelinye, isib. guda, duda
Ukuphula amagama amade abe zincunjwana ezincani, isib. Umu-tjho, ngo-mba-na, pha-na-ge-la
 IMITHETHO YOKUPELEDA
Ukutlola imithetho yokupeleda

 22 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
3.2 UKWENDLALWA KWAMATHEKSTHI EMAGREYIDINI 4-6

Imihlobo ehlukahlukeneko yamatheksthi ikhethelwe elinye nelinye ihlandla leemveke ezimbili. Amatheksthi
eqakathekileko, athethwe ethebulini 3.1 anikelwe ngenzasi. Abafundi bazokufunda ngamanye nofana ngawo woke
amatheksthi ehlandlni leemveke ezimbili, okutjho bona bazokulalela nofana bethule ngomlomo nofana bafunde
nofana batlole itheksthi. Koke ukufunda nokufundisa kumele kudzimelele ematheksthini lawa, kunqotjhiswe
ekuthuthukiseni amakghono begodu namakghonywana welimi njengombana ivezwe ngenzasi.

Ithebula elingenzasi lisirhunyezo samatheksthi afakwe komunye nomunye umzombe weemveke ezimbili eHlelweni
lokuFundisa elisesiGabeni 3.5.

3.3 AMAHLELO WOKUFUNDISA

Ihlelo lokufundisa litjengisa ubuncani bokumumethweko okufanele kwenziwe qobe ngeemveke ezimbili ethemini.
Ukulandelana/ukulamana kokumumethweko okurhelisiweko akukaqintelwa begodu nesikhathi esinikelweko pheze
sisitjengiso sokobana okumumethweko kufanele kwenziwe ngesikhathi esingangani. Abotitjhere bafanele batlame
amaHlelo womSebenzi/amaTjhejula basebenzisa amahlelo wokufundisa, iincwadi zabo ezitlolelwe ukufundisa
isifundo esithileko/amatheksthibhugu wabo nezinye iinsiza ukufundisa okumumethweko ngokusebenzisa
ukulandelana nebelo elifaneleko. Abotitjhera bayakhuthazwa bonyana basebenzise godu okumumethweko nofana
imiqondo esebujameni bebhoduluko labo.

3.3.1 Indlela amatheski ahlobana ngayo emzombeni weemveke ezimbili

Amatheksthi ahlukahlukeneko asetjenziswe njengesisekelo sokutlama umzombe wokufundisa weemveke ezimbili.
Akhethwe njengesisekelo esitjengisa bonyana ahlobana bunjani ndawonye ukwakha iyunithi ehlangeneko,
isibonelo, abafundi bazokulalela indatjana bese bayayifunda. Bazokubawiwa bonyana batlole ihlathululo efitjhani
yomlomo yendawo nofana yomuntu (ezokuhlobana nendatjana) nofana bangabawiwa bonyana batlolele umlingisi
osendatjaneni incwadi. Khetha ummongo/isihloko somzombe omunye nomunye weemveke ezimbili ozokukwenza
ukwazi ukuhlobanisa imisebenzi ngepumelelo. Isizathu sokusebenzisa imimmongo/iinhloko kukobana kubuyelelwe
kusetjenziswe godu ilwazimagama begodu nezakhiwo zelimi ebujameni obuzwakalako.

3.3.2 Indlela amatheksthi/imisebenzi ilandelaniswa ngayo ngozombe weemveke ezimbili

Amatheski akukafaneli afundiswe ngendlela ethileko. Ebujameni obunengi, kufanele kube nomsebenzi/itheski
eLalelwako neKhulunywako ukulungiselela umsebenzi wokufunda nofana wokutlola. Kwesinye isikhathi, umsebenzi
wokuLalela nokuKhuluma kufanele ususelwe etheksthini yokufunda. Abafundi bafanele bazibandakanye emihlotjeni
ehlukahlukeneko yamatheksthi ngomlomo begodu nangokufunda ngaphambi kobana babawiwe bonyana batlole
amatheksthi lawo. Esikhathini esinengi, itheksthi efanele ilalelwe, isib. indatjana nofana umbiko weendaba
uzokwehluka begodu ube sezingeni eliphezulu kunalawo abafundi abazowafunda. Lokhu kubangelwa kukobana
amakghono wabo wokulalela athuthuke khulu kunamakghono wabo wokufunda.

3.3.3 Imihlobo yamatheksthi eqintelweko nephakanyisiweko

Imihlobo yamatheksthi aqintelweko afanele afundiswe komunye nomunye umzombe weemveke ezimbili inikelwe
ehlelweni lokuhlola begodu ifanele ibe khona eencwadini ezitlolelwe ukufundisa isifundo esithileko/amatheksthibhugu

 34 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
aqintelweko. Ukuzikhethela kungenziwa emihlotjeni ehlukehlukeneko yeendatjana zesikhathi sanje, iindatjana
zokuzicabangela (isib. isehlakalo, eziyingozi, indatjana yesayensi ezingasiliqiniso) iindatjana zomlando (isib. imilando
ngamaphilo wabantu) begodu neendatjana zendabuko (isib. iinolwana eziphathelene nezekolo(imithi), iinolwana-
mlando (amalejendi)iinolwana eziphathelene neembandana) ezikhona. Lokhu kuliqiniso nangeenkondlo nemidlalo.

Kukhona nesigaba sesihloko esithi “Ukuzindla ngamatheksthi afundwe ngesikhathi sokufunda ngokuzijamela
nofana ngababili.” Lokhu kuzokuba matheksthi akhethwe encwadini yokufunda/iincwadi zokufunda/nakezinye
iincwadi(ukufunda okungezelelweko) begodu azokusekela amatheksthi afundweko esigabeni esiqintelweko. Kungaba
mihlobo efanako yamatheksthi (ukuqinisekisa ukuzwisisa isakhiwo setheksthi) nofana imihlobo ehlukahlukeneko
yamatheksthi (ukuhlahlambisa ikareko eliragela phambili begodu nokungezelela imihlobo ehlukahlukeneko
yamakghono wokufunda). Kibo boke ubujamo, ukufunda lokhu okungeziweko kufanele kukhambisane neenhloko
nemimmongo ekhethelwe amatheksthi aqintelweko emzombeni lowo weemveke ezimbili. Lokhu kuqakatheke khulu
njengombana kuhlanganisa ilwazi nokuzwisisa ilwazi-magama nemiqondo ephathelene nesihloko kwabafundi
beLimi lokuThoma lokweNgeza.Kufanele kuyelelwe bonyana umnqopho kukwenza abafundi bafunde ngokuzijamela
begodu bathuthukise ukuzijayeza ukufunda. Ngesikhathi sokufunda ngayedwana, abantwana bafanele banikelwe
ithuba lokufunda iincwadi ezikhambisana nekareko labo begodu bazindle ngalokho abakufundileko.

3.3.4 Inani lamatheksthi aqakathekileko ngomzombe weemveke ezimbili

Engcenyeni yokuthoma yonyaka, ngokujayelekileko kunomhlobo munye tere wetheksthi nofana umsebenzi ngokuya
ngomzombe weemveke ezimbili. Ngokukhamba kwesikhathi, kuzokuba namibili begodu kwesinye isikhathi kube
nemihlobo nofana imisebenzi emithathu ngomzombe weemveke ezimbili. Fundisa imihlobo le yamatheksthi
begodu nemisebenzi ehlobene nayo ngokuyelela bonyana abafundi bazokwethulelwa wona amahlandla amanengi
emnyakeni woke.

3.3.5 Indlela okwethulwa ngayo iZakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yokuSetjenziswa kweLimi

Okumumethweko esigabeni “seZakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yokusetjenziswa kweLimi” kuhlobene esikhathini esinengi
nemihlobo yamatheksthi aqintelweko ngaphasi kweenhloko: ukuLalela nokuKhuluma, ukuFunda nokuBukela,
begodu nokuTlola nokwEthula, begodu kuzokutjhejwa endleleni yekambiso yokuzibandakanya namatheksthi
begodu nangesikhathi esabelwe ukuLalela nokuKhuluma, ukuFunda nokuBukela, ukuTlola nokwEthula. Isibonelo,
nakwenziwa indatjana, ngokujayelekileko abafundi bazokusebenzisa isikhathi esilula esidlulileko begodu
bazokufunda amatheksthi basebenzisa isikhathi lesi. Nanyana kunjalo, kuqakathekile kobana kutlanywe imisebenzi
etjheja izakhiwo zelimi, ebujameni obuthileko. Khetha ama-ayithemu esigabeni “seZakhiwo nemiThetjhwana
yokusetjenziswa kweLimi ” ukufundisa abafundi ilimi elivela ngokwemvelo emihlotjeni yetheksthi okunqotjhiswe
kiyo begodu ukwenzelela ukusekela indlela yemvelo nokulamanisa ukuthola ilimi. Akusiwo woke ama-ayithemu
okufanele afundiswe emzombeni onikelweko kodwana qinisekisa bonyana zoke izinto/ama-ayithemu arhenyisiweko
embonweni-mazombe ayafundwa emnyakeni woke.

Tlama imisebenzi ezwakalako ebafundini nekhambisana namatheksthi abawafundako emzombeni weemveke
ezimbili. Imisebenzi eminengi yomhlobo lo kufanele yenziwe njengombana abafundi baphumelela ukusuka kuGreyidi
4 ukufika kuGreyidi 6. Khetha ngokuyelela imithetho oyihlathululela abafundi begodu ungenzi bona ibe minengi.
Abafundi beLimi lokuThoma lokwEngeza bafunda ilimi ngokuvezwa kilo njalonjalo nangokulisebenzisa. Ukuzijayeza
izakhiwo nokusetjenziswa kwelimi kuzokwenza ngcono amakghono lawa. Ukufundisa kufanele kuhlanganise woke
amakghono welimi nezakhiwo zelimi njengombana zihlobana. Koke lokhu kufanele kufundiswe ebujameni obuthileko.
Yelela bonyana kunesikhathi esinqophileko esabelwe imilayelo ehlelekileko eZakhiweni nemiThetjhwaneni
yokuSetjenziswa kweLimi.

 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
ISIGABA 4: UKUHLOLA

4.1 ISINGENISO

Ukuhlola yindlela yekambiso eragela phambili ehleliweko yokubona, ukubuthelela nokuhlathulula ilwazi ngokusebenza
kwabafundi . Kufaka hlangana amagadango amane, kusetjenziswa imihlobohlobo ehlukeneko yokuhlola: ukukhiqiza
nokubuthelela ubufakazi, ukurekhoda okufunyenweko nokusebenzisa ilwazi lokuzwisisa ukuze kusizwe ukuthuthuka
komfundi ukuthuthukisa indlela yekambiso yokufunda nokufundisa.

Ukuhlola kufanele kube ngokungakahleleki (ukuhlolela ukufunda) nokuhlelekileko (ukuhlola kokufundiweko). Kikho
kokubili ukuhlola, kufanele abafundi banikelwe umbiko obuyako njalonjalo khona kuzokungezelelwa ilemuko labo
lokufunda

Eemfundweni zamaLimi ukuhlola kuragela phambili begodu kusekela ukukhula netuthuko yabafundi. Kuyincenye
yokufundisa nokufunda njengombana kunikela umbiko obuyako ngokufunda nokufundisa. Kufanele kuhlanganiswe
ekufundiseni nokufundeni kunokobana kwenziwe njengento ezijameleko.Ukuyaphambili, ukuhlola okuhlanganisiweko
kwemihlobohlobo ehlukeneko yelimi kufanele kujayezwe.Isibonelo, singathoma ngokufunda isiqetjhana bese kuhlolwa
ukuzwisisa.Imibuzo yelwazi leLimi ingenziwa isuselwa etheksthini yonaleyo efundiweko.Ngemva kokufunda itheksthi
abafundi bangabawiwa bonyana baphendule ngetheksthi, isibonelo, ukutlola incwadi ngezinto ezivezwe etheksthini
nayana ukutlola ezinye iimpendulo ngokumumethwe yitheksthi. Ukusonga umsetjenzana, yokucocisana kungasiza
ngesihloko begodu ngalendlela kungethulwa woke amakghono welimi ngomsetjenzana owodwa otjhelelako
ohlanganisiweko.

Ukuhlola amakghono ahlukeneko welimi akukafaneli kubonwe njengomsetjenzana ozijameleko kodwana umsebenzi
owodwa ohlanganisiweko. Amarubhrigi wokuhlola afanele ethule amakghono ahlukahlukeneko welimi asethaskini.

Amakghono wabafundi wokomlomo, ikghono lokuphendula imibuzo, ukuzibandakanya eengcocweni nemakghonweni
wamarekhodi atloliweko nakukghonakalako angatjhejwa ngamalanga.

Kuqakathekile, godu, bonyana ukuzwisisa kwabafundi ngalokho abakufundako kuyahlolwa begodu ingasi ikghono
labo lokukhumbula nofana ukuphula amagama. Ngakho-ke ukuhlolwa kokufunda kufanele kwenzeke ngamalanga
kungabi nje kuhlola okwenzeka ngakanye. Ukuhlola ukufunda okuhlelekileko kufanele kunqophe ekufundeleni
phezulu nemisetjenzaneni ezakusiza ekuboneni bonyana abafundi bazwisise kangangani, isibonela, ukubuyelela
acoce indatjana nofana aphendule imibuzo.

Ukuhlolwa komsebenzi otloliweko kuzokunqopha ekghonweni labafundi lokudlulisa ihlathululo, begodu nokuthi
litloleke kuhle ngokufaneleko, isibonelo, ukusebenzisa izakhiwo nokusetjenziswa kwelimi ngefanelo, ukupeleda
netshwayo. Koke ukuhlola kufanele kubonelele bonyana ukufundwa kwelimi kuyindlela yekambiso nokuthi ekuthomeni
abafundi bangekhe bakhiqize isiqetjhana somsebenzi esilungileko. Ngakho-ke iingaba ezahlukahlukeneko zeendlela
yekambiso yokutlola zifanele zihlolwe nazo.

Lokha nawunikela ithaski ehlelekileko yokuhlola, kuzokuba nomnqopho wekghono elithile.isibonelo, UkuLalela
nokuKhuluma namkha ukuFunda nofana ukuTlola.Nanyana kunjalo ngombana ukufunda ilimi.kuyindlela yekambiso
ehlanganisiweko, kuzokusetjenziswa ikghono elingaphezulu kelilodwa.Izakhiwo zelimi zifanele zihlolwe ebujameni
obuthile. Kufanele kuqinisekiswe bonyana ukuhlola akwenziwa emsebenzini otlolwako kwaphela, kodwana vumela
umsebenzi owenziwako newokomlomo. Kuqakathekile ukuhlola lokho abafundi abakwaziko ingasi lokho abakwazi
ngehloko kwaphela, ngakho-ke hlola amakghono ebujameni obuthile ngendlela okungakghonakala ngayo, isib,
abafundi bangahle bapelede woke amagama ngefanelo ngeLesihlanu ngesikhathi sokuhlolwa, kodwana bayakghona

 126 ISITATIMENDE SOMTHETHO-KAMBISO WEKHARIKHYULAMU NOKUHLOLA (TTKH)
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
yini ukusebenzisa wona amagamalawo apeledwe kuhle ngesikhathi sokutlola/nebarekhoda iindaba ekungezabo
nofana iindatjana?

Ukufundisa nokuhlolwa kwelimi kufanele kunikele ithuba lokufaka boke abafundi, nomaqhinga afanele
atholakale ukusiza boke abafundi ukuthola nofana ukukhiqiza amatheksi welimi.Abanye babafundi abaneenqabo
abangekhe bakghone ukuthola eminye yeminqopho njengombana yethulwe esiTatimendeni somThetho-kambiso
weKharikhyulamu nokuHlola.

Abafundi beLimi lokuThoma lokweNgeza ukuphumelela kwabo kudzimelele ekuhloleni okwenzeka kanengi, ukuhlola
okungakahlelwa nombiko obuyako ovela kutitjhere njengobana bazibandakanya namatheksthi nemisetjenzana.
Ihlelo lokufundisa lethula okumumethweko okufunekako, amakghono namaqhinga wokufundisa nokufunda konyaka.
Ngilokhu ekuzakuhlowa emazingeni wokuthuthuka akhulako (Ukuragela phambili), . Ingasi yoke into efundiswako
kufuze ihlolwe ngokuhlola okuhlelekileko. kodwana, kuqakathekile kobana ipumelelo yabafundi ihlolwe ngehlelo
elihlelekileko nelingakahlelwa, njengobana lokhu kuveza amalungiselela wokuplana(ukulungiselelwa komsebenzi)..

Ihlelo lokuhlola livumela ukuhlola okurhunyeziweko, okungathatha ubujamo bokuhlola namkha ukuhlahluba,
ekupheleni kwayo yoke ithemu. Umsebenzi ohlolwako kufanele kube ngiloyo owenziwe ethemu leyo. Izinto
ezizokuhlolwa zifanele zibekwe emazingeni welwazi elihlukahlukeneko ukuqinisekisa ubuqiniso.

4.2 UKUHLOLA OKUNGAKAHLELEKI NOFANA KWANGAMALANGA

Ukuhlolela ukufunda kunomnqopho oragela phambili wokubuthelela ilwazi lokuphumelela komfundi elingasetjenziselwa
ukwenza ngcono ukufunda.

Ukuhlola okungakahleleki kukutjheja iragelo phambili labafundi langamalanga. Lokhu kwenziwa ngokutjheja, iingcoco,
ukubonisa ngokwenza, ukucocisana phakathi komfundi notitjhere, ukuthintana kwangetlasini okungakahleleki, njll.
Ukuhlola abafundi okungakahleleki kungabalula njengokujama utjheje abafundi ngesikhathi sesifundo, namkha
ukucocisana nabafundi ngeragelo phambili labo lokufunda, Ukuhlolwa kwekghono lelimi lizokuba ngendlela
yokutjheja, umsebenzi wokuhlola, imisetjezana yokomlomo nokwethula, ukuhlola okutlolwako, ukufundela phezulu
nezinye iindlela zokuhlola.Ukuhlola okungakahleleki kufanele kusetjanziselwe ukunikela umbiko obuyako ebafundini
nokulungiselela ihlelo(iplani) lokufundisa, nokho akutlhogeki bonyana kurekhodwe.Akukafaneli bonyana kubonwe
njengomsetjenzana ohlukeneko kileyo misetjenzana yokufunda eyenzeke ngetlasini begodu ungasebenzisa
imisetjenzana eminengi yokufunda ukuhlola ukusebenza komfundi okungakahleleki. Ngesinge isikhathi, ungahle
ufune ukubeka umhlobo wokuhlola okunqophileko wemisetjenzana ukugqugquzela abafundi bakho bonyana
bafunde, njengekuhlola njalonjalo ukupeleda .Abafundi nanyana utitjhere bangatshwaya amathaski wokuhlola lawo.

Kutjhukunyiswe bonyana usebenzise iimveke ezimbili zokuthoma zethemu ukwenza ukuhlolwa kwabafundi
okusisekelo.Kufanele usebenzise imisetjenzana enikelwe eemvekeni ezimbili zokuthoma zehlelo lokufundisa
ukwenza ukuhlola lokho.Lokhu kuzokusiza bonyana uthole ihlobo letjhejo elidingwa bafundi bakho njengombana
uzabe uragela phambili.

Imisetjenzana yokuzihlola neyokuhlolwa mlingani ibandakanya abafundi ekuhloleni. Lokhu kuqakathekile
njengombana kuvumela abafundi ukufunda nokuzindla ngokudlulileko ngokusebenza kwabo. Imiphumela yamathaski
wokuhlola ukuhlola kwangamalanga okungakahlelelwa awarekhodwa ngokuhlelekileko ngaphandle kobana utitjhere
afise ukwenza njalo.Abotitjhere bangahle bafise ukugqina amarekhodi wabo angakahleleki ngeragelo phambili
lomfundi ngamunye ezintweni ezihlukeneko zesifundo ukusiza ngokuhlela namkha ukuqinisekisa bonyana umfundi
ngamunye uthuthukisa amakghono nokuzwisisa okulindelekileko. imiphumela yamathaski wokuhlola yangamalanga
ayisetjenziselwa ukudluliselwa esigabeni esilandelako nofana ukutholwa kwesitifiketi.

TTKH 127
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
4.3 UKUHLOLA OKUHLELEKILEKO

Yoke imisebenzi yokuhlola ehlekileko eyakha ihlelo lokuhlola okuhlekileko kiloyo nyaka ithathwa njengokuhlola
okuhlelekiko. Amathaski wokuhlola ahlelekileko atshwaywa bewarekhodwe ngokuhlelekileko ngutitjhere, ngehloso
yokuwasebenzisela ukuragela phambili komfundi. Woke amathaski wokuhlola ahlelekileko angasetjenziselwa
ukulinganisa ngomnqopho wokuqinisekisa ikhwalithi begodu nokuqinisekisa bonyana amathaski ahlala asezingeni
elifaneleko.

Ukuhlola okuhlelekiko kunikela utiitjhere indlela yangokomthetho yokuhlola iragelo phambili labafundi egreyidini
nesifundwani esithileko. Iimbonelo zokuhlola okuhlelekiko kufaka hlangana ukuhlola, iinhlahlubo, amathaski
wokwenza, amaphrojekthi, ukwethula ikulumo yomlomo, ukutjengisa, (okufana nokubuyelela indatjana, ukumetjhisa),
ukulingisa (okufana nokwenza) iindatjana, ukuzibandakanya ematheksthini womlomo(afana nekulumo pikiswano,
ukucocisana, ingcoco)amatheksthi atloliweko(okufana nokuqedelela iphepha lokusebenzela, ukutlola iindinyana
nofana eminye imihlobo yamatheksthi), njll

Ihloso yokutlama ihlelo lokuhlola kukuqinisekisa ubuqiniso, ukuthembeka, ukungasolisiko nangokwaneleko
kokuhlolwa ngokunikela umhlahlo ohlathululwe kabanzi ngemihlobo yemisetjenzana namaphesende anikelwe elinye
nelinye ikghono lelimi elisethaskini.Begodu lithula umnqopho wokuhlola, o.u.ngendlela ithaski okufanele lethulwe
ngayo.

Ekuhlolweni okuhlelekileko, sebenzisa amamemorandamu, amarubhrigi, irhelo wokuhlola nesikala sokulinganisa
begodu nezinye iintlabagelo zokuhlola ezifanele ukuthjeja, ukuhlola nokurekhoda izinga labafundi lokuzwisisa
namakghono. Khetha isitlabagelo sokutlola esifanele ukuhlola esifane umhlobo othile womsebenzi. Isibonelo,
irubhrigi ifanele khulu ukusetjenziselwa isiqetjhana sokutlola ngokuzitlamela.Imemorandamu ifanele khulu ekuhloleni
ukupeleda nofana umsetjenzana wesiqephu sokuzwisisa.

4.3.1 Iimfuneko zokuhlola okuhlelekileko zeLimi Lekhaya

Ihlelo lokuhlola okuhlelekileko lamaGreyidi 4-6 lakhiwa ngamathaski alikhomba (7) enza 75% yamamaksi wokuragela
phambili neenhlahlubo zinye zekupheleni konyaka okuyi 25%.

Iimfuneko zokuhlola okuhlelekileko zeLimi lokuThoma lokweNgeza ngilezi ezilandelako:

• Amathaski wokuhlola okuhlelekileko amabili afanele enziwe kenye nenye ithemu emathemini amathathu
 wokuthoma. BESE ibe yinye ngethemu yesine.Enye yamathaski ifanele ibe kuhlahluba/ziinhlahlubo
 kwaphakathi konyaka.Inani lamathaski ahlelekileko afanele abe likhomba.Amathaski alikhomba lawa enza
 75% yesamba samamaksi we Limi Lekhaya emaGreyidini 4, 5 no 6.Amamaksi wokuhlola okuhlelekileko
 afaka hlangana iinhlahlubo zaphakathi konyaka.

• Amathaski wokuthoma wokuhlola okuhlelekileko ethemeni ngayinye afanele enziwe phakathi kwethemu.
 lokhu kutjho ukuthi ngeveke yesihlanu.Ithaski yesibili ehlelekileko ifanele yenziwe ngasekupheleni
 kwethemu.

• Kuzokuba neenhlahlubo zokuphela konyaka ezizokubala 25% yamamaksi

• Enye nenye ithaski yokuhlola ukuhlola okuhlelekileko ifanele yenziwe ngemisetjenzana ehlola ukuLalela
 nokuKhuluma, ukuFunda nokuBukela, ukuTlola nokweThula, neZakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yeLimi, begodu
 kufanele yenziwe ngamalanga ambalwa.Izakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yeLimi ifanele ihlolwe ebujameni obuthile.

 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
• Amathaski wokuhlola okuhlelekileko afanele ahlole imihlobohlobo yamakghono welimi ukwenzela bonyana
 izinto eziqakathekileko zizokuhlolwa iinkhathi ezinengi ethemini nangonyaka.Qinisekisa bonyana izinto lezo
 zathoma zahlolwa ekuhloleni okungakahleleki nabafundi banikelwa umbiko obuyako ngaphambili kobana
 zihlolwe ngokuhlelekileko

• Koke ukuhlolwa esiGabeni esiPhakathi kwenziwa ngaphakathi/esikolweni.

4.3.2 Imihlobo yamathaski ngethemu

Imihlobo yokuhlola esetjenziswako ifanele ibe sezingeni lobudala nokuthuthuka okufaneleko.Ukutlanywa kwamathaski
kufanele kufake koke okumumethweko ngesifundo begodu kufake hlangana imihlobohlobo yamathaski atlanyelwe
ukuphumelelisa iinhloso zesifundo. Zifanele zisekelwe elwazini nemakghonweni enziwe ngethemu lelo. Sebenzisa
iplani lethemu legreyidi ngayinye ukukhetha imihlobo yemisetjenzana namasede wamakghono afunekako wenye
nenye ingcenye yethaski lokuhlola okuhlelekileko.Isibonelo, nange uhlela indinyana yokutlola ngokutlamela eGreidini
4, ngethemu 1 bese ufuna abafundi batlole ikondlo, ungalindela ukuthola bonyana ‘batlole imitjho yobude obulinganako
nelinevumelwano’, njengambana lokho ngilokho ozabe sele ukufundisile.Nange uhlela itheksthi yelwazi ngethemu
yokuthoma, abafundi bazokulindeleka bonyana batlole basebenzisa ifremu efaneleko.Ngokunjalo nangokuLalela
nokuKhuluma angekhe ubawe abafundi bonyana banikele ingcoco efitjhani eGreyidini yesi-4 njengombana lokho
kuzokufundiswa ekugcineni.

Ukuhlola okuhlelekileko kufanele kubonelele imihlobohlobo yamazinga njengombana zitjengiswe ngaphasi.
Imihlobohlobo yemihlobo yemibuzo efana nemibuzo yokuzikhethela (multiple choice) yokuzalisa (cloze)
yokumadanisa(comparison) nenqophileko(direct) ifanele isetjenziswe.

 • Ingabe ukuphikisa komtloli kunobuqiniso/kuyalandelana/kuyaphetha.
 • Coca/phawula ngokuhlaba ngesenzo/ngehloso//umnqopho/indlela
 yokucabanga/isitjhukumezo/ukuthinteka kokuthile
 • Uyavumelana nombono/nesitatimende/ukutjheja/ukurhumutjha bonyana…
 • Ngombono wakho, umtloli/umcoci/umlingisi uyaqinisa ngokutjhukumisa/
 ngokujamisa ukuthi…
 • (jamiselela ipendulo yakho/nikela iinzathuzependulo yakho)
 • Ingabe isenzo/ummoya womuntu/iminqopho yomlingisi iveza ini ebujameni Amazinga 4 no
 beendawo zoke?
 5:20%
 • Ukucoca ngokuhlaba/ukuphawula ngobungako bokwahlulela okwenziwe
 etheksthini.

 Imibuzo le ihlose ukuhlola ukubethana ngokomkhumbulo nabuthanda
 Ukubuka buhle betheksthi emfundini. Inqophe eempendulweni zokuphakama
 (Izinga 5) kwemizwa kokumumethweko, kufaniswa nabalingisi nofana izehlakalo,
 nendlela okuphendulwe ngayo ilimi ngomtloli elisetjenziswa mtloli (okufana
 nokukhethwa kwegama nangokufanekisa)
 • Coca ngependulo yakho ngetheksthi/ngezehlakalo/ngobujamo/ngokurarana/
 ngobujamo bokukhetha ezintweni ezimbili ongazithandiko.
 • Uyazwelana nomlingisi?Ngisiphi isenzo/isiqunto ongabe usithethe nengabe
 nawe bowusebujameni obufana nalobu?
 • Coca/phawula ngelimi elisetjenziswe mtloli…
 • Coca ngendlela ephumelelako yendlela/isingeniso/isiphetho/isifanekiso/
 isingathekiso/ukusebenzisa amaqhinga wobukondlo/iinsiza eziphathelene
 nezemitlolo zomtloli…

4.4 IHLELO LOKUHLOLA

Ihlelo lokuhlola litlanyelwe ukunabisa yoke imisebenzi yokuhlola okuhlelekileko eemfundweni zoke esikolweni
bekuphele ithemu. Ihlelo lokuhlola kufuze litlanywe esikolweni sitjengise amalanga lapho amathaski azakwenziwa
khona

Iimfuneko zesifundo

Iimfuneko zokwakha ithaski zinikelwe ngokuya ngamapesende.Lapho ihlelo litjengisa amaphesende ama-
20 wekghono lelimi kutjho ukuthi ukunikelwa kokugcina kwamamaksi wekghono lelimi kufanele kube sisamba
samaphesende ama-20 ingasi amamaksi amatjhumi amabili.Iinkolo azikabekelwa umkhawulo wenani lamamaksi
abelwe ikghono lelimi kwaphela nange isilinganiso sekghono selinye nelinye ikghono sitjhejwa lelinye nelinye
ithaski ngokuya ngamaphesende abiweko asehlelweni lokuhlola.Isibonelo, eGreyidini yesi-4.ukuhlola ilwazi lelimi
ingahlelelwa amamaksi ama-20 nofana ngaphezulu, kwaphela nange isilinganiso singeze sadlula isilinganiso
esitjengiswe ehlelweni lokuhlola.

TTKH 131
 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
Emahlangothini wokutlola ihlelo lendlela yekambiso nofana yoke indlela yekambiso ifanele ihlolwe okungasenani
kanye ngethemu.Ubude bamatheksthi njengombana butjengiswe eSigabeni 3.3 kufanele kulandelwe njengombana
bunjalo.

Iinhlahlubo

Okumumethweko kwekupheleni konyaka kufanela kukhitjhwe emsebenzini wonyaka begodu kufanele kukhethwe
emakghonweni nemisetjenzaneni ezokuvumela/ezokwenza umfundi bonyana atjengise ukuthi ukulungele
ukuzibandakanya nomsebenzi wonyaka olandelako

Iinhlahlubo zizokwakhiwa/zizokuba nalokhu okulandelako;

• Indinyana efundwako, okufaka hlangana nomsebenzi welwazimagama

• Ukutlolwa kwetheksthi efitjhani etlanyiweko, efakahlangana ukusetjenziswa kwesakhiwo, ihlelo, itshwayo
 nokupeleda ngefanelo.

• Ukutlola itheksthi yokuthintana efitjhani, efakahlangana ukusetjenziswa ngefanelo isakhiwo, ihlelo, itshwayo
 nokupeleda.

• Izakhiwo nemiThetjhwana yeLimi ukutjengisa ilwazi nokuzwisisa ihlelo, itshwayo nokupeleda

• Amakghono wokuLalela noKukhuluma awasoze ahlolwe njengengcenye yeenhlahlubo njengombana
 zona zihloleka kangcono nazihlolwa isikhathi eside. Nanyana kunjalo, kulindelekile bonyana amamaksi
 wokuhlola okuphethako, asekelwe ekuhloleni okuhlelekileko enzelwe ukuLalela nokuKhuluma, azokunikelwa
 njengamamaksi weenhlahlubo.

Amathebula alandelako anikela iimfuneko zokuhlolwa okuhlelekileko kweLimi lokuThoma lokweNgeza:

4.5 UKUREKHODA NOKUBIKA

Ukurekhoda yindlela yekambiso esetjenziswa ngutitjhere nakabuthelela ubufakazi ngezinga lokusebenza
komfundi emsebenzini wokuhlola. Ukurekhoda kuveza iragelo phambili lomfundi ekuthumbeni ilwazi eliqintelweko
esiTatimendeni somGomo weKharikhyulamu nokuHlola. Amarekhodi wokusebenza kwabafundi kufanele aveze
ubufakazi beragelo phambili labafundi ngokwengqondo kileyo greyidi nokulungela ukudlulela egreyidini elandelako.
Amarekhodi wokusebenza kwabafundi kufanele asetjenziselwe ukuqinisekisa iragelo phambili likatitjhere nabafundi
ekufundeni nekufundiseni

Ukubika yindlela yokwazisa ngokusebenza komfundi ebafundini, ababelethi, iinkolo nabanye abadlali ndima.
Ukusebenza kwabafundi kungabikwa ngeendlela ezihlukahlukeneko. Ukubika kufaka hlangana amakarada
wokubika, imihlangano yababelethi, amalanga wokuvakajthela isikolo, amakhomferensi wababelethi nabotitjhere,
iphepha-bhugu lesikolo, nokhunye. Abotitjhere bamagreyidi woke basebenzisa amazinga ali-7 ahlathululako kanye
namaphesende esifundweni ngasinye ukusukela kuGreyidi R – 12, qala ithebula elandelako

AMAKHOWUDU NAMAPHESENDE WOKUREKHODA NOKUBIKA^

 IKHOWUDU YAMANANI UKUHLATHULULWA KWEKGHONO AMAPHESENDE
 7 Uphumelele kuhle tle 80 – 100
 6 Uphumelele ngendlela ethokozisako 70 – 79
 5 Uphumelele ngamandla 60 – 69
 4 Uphumelele ngokwaneleko 50 – 59
 3 Uphumelele ngokulingeneko 40 – 49
 2 Uphumelele ngendlela ephasi 30 – 39
 1 Akakaphumeleli 0 - 29

Yelela: Isikali samaphuzu ali-7 kufanele sibe neenhlathululo ezizwakalako ezinikela ilwazi elizeleko ezingeni ngalinye.

Abotitjhere bazakurekhoda amamaksi womsebenzi ephepheni lokurekhoda bese babika ngokwamaphesende
esifundweni ngasinye ekaradeni lomfundi lokubika.

4.6 UKULINGANISA UKUHLOLA

Ukulinganisa yikambiso eqinisekisa bona imisebenzi yokuhlola ayithathi hlangothi, inobuqiniso nokuthembeka.
Ukulinganisa kufanele kusetjenziswe eenkolweni, eemfundeni, ephrovinsini nemazingeni welizweloke. Kufanele
kusetjenziswe ukulinganisa okuzeleko nokufaneleko khona kuzokuqinisekiswa ikhwalithi eemfundweni zoke. Lokhu
kungenziwa kanye ngethemu.

Ukulinganisa kufanele kuqinisekise bonyana koke ukuhlola akuthathi hlangothi, kunobuqiniso nokuthembeka begodu
kungokwaneleko. Ubuqiniso buhlathulula bonyana ithaski kufanele ulinganise iinzuzo yamakghono afundisiweko
ngokwesiTatimende soMgomo weKharikhyulamu nokuHlola. Ithaski kufanele ilinganise izinga lokuphumelela
amakghono athileko. Lokha nakutlanywa imibuzo yokuzwisisa isibonelo, kuhlolwa ikghono lomfundi lokutsenga
nokuhIanganisa ilwazi elisetheksthini, kungabi mibuzo ejayelekileko. Imibuzo mayibe ngezwakalako, edzimelele
etheksthini kufane ihlolwe .nenobuqiniso beyihlole ilwazi lelimi nehlelo. Begodu kungabuzwa imibuzo ngelwazi
elijayelekileko elihlobana netheksthi lifanele lihlolwe.

Abalinganisi bokuhlola emikhakheni yeenkolweni kufanele banikele ngokuphawula okunekhwalithi ukuqinisekisa
kobana ukuzijayeza ukuhlola eenkolweni kuyakhuliswa. Ukuhlela angeze kwaba msebenzi wokubeka ilihlo wokuhlola

 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
kobana isibalo esifaneleko semisebenzi senziwe nofana imemorandamu isetjenzise kuhle. Emalimini kutjho bonyana
umlinganisi uzakuphawula ngefanelo, phakathi kwezinye izinto, emikhakheni yokubuza ngokuhlola kokuzwisisa;
ukutlola okwenzeka kanengi; ikhwalithi yeensetjenziswa zokuhlola begodi namathuba wokuthuthuka afumanekako,
begodu nokuzibandakanya kukatitjhere ngokusenzisa incwadi yomfundi nobufakazi bokusebenza ngepumelelo
komfundi.

Ikambiso yokulinganisa ukuhlola kufuze iqinisekise kobana ukukala okunikelweko kuyafana kiwo woke amatlasi
egreyidii begodu nakiwo woke amagreyidi asesigabeni, . Isibonelo, ukukala kwanga-3 okunikelwa ngititjhere munye
kufuze kujamele izinga elifanako lekghono nelwazi ngokufanako nezinga elinikelwe ngomunye utitjhere. Ngalokho ke
kuqakathekile kwiimphathiswa ezizinhloko zeemfundo kobana zenze ukuhlela kwangaphakathi kanengi.

IGLOZARI

I-akhronimi/ibizo-sirhunyezwa- ligama elibizekako, elakhiwa ngamaledere wokuthoma wegama (isbisib. FET-
Further Education and Training)

ilimi lokwEngeza- (Qala ilimi lekhaya)- ngelinye ilimi elifundwa mfundi ngaphandle kwelimi lakhe lekhaya

ubulimi-nengi bokungezelela: umuntu lokha nakafunda ilimi (nanyana amalimi) ukungezelela elimini lakhe lekhaya.
Ilimi leli alithathi indawo yelimi lekhaya kodwana lifundwa kanye kanye nelimi lekhaya. Ehlelweni lobulimi-nengi, ilimi
lekhaya liyaqinisekiswa ngesikhathi lelo lokungezelela lithathwa njengelinye lesibili elisekelako (isib.woke amalimi
wokungezelelwa, kufakwe hlangana ilimi lokuFunda nokuFundisa nawo afundiswa kunye nelimi lekhaya kodwana
akalijamiseleli).

thanda ubuhle belimi– Lokha umuntu afunda ilimi (nofana amalimi) ukwengezelela elimini okungelakhe namkha
iLimi Lekhaya .Ilimi leli alingezeleli ilimi lekhaya kodwana lifundwa ngokukhambisana nalo..Emahlelweni wethanda
buhle belimi, ilimi lekhaya liyangezelelwa beliqinisekiswe ngesikhathi nanyana ngiliphi ilimi elingezelelako lifundwa
libonwa njengakungezelela ukuqakatheka kwelimi lelo elifundwako(isib. Woke amaLimi woKwengezelela okufaka
hlangana iLimiLokufundisa, afundisaw akhambisane neLimi Lekhaya nokho angalijamiseleli)

ukungahlathululeki kuhle- ukuhlathulula okubili okwenzeke ekusetjenzisweni kwegama nawulisebenzisa
ungakatjheji, ukungahlathululeki kuhle kutjhugulula umqondo (isib. Ubaba uthi ingahlatjwa ikomo emhlophe)

Umqondophika-igama eliphikisanako ngehlathululo egameni elinye iLimini elifanako (isib.’ukujabula’ ‘nokudana’)

Ukuhlola-isakhiwo sendlela yekambiso eragela phambili yokubuthelela ilwazi ngekhgono lomfundi ngeendlela
ezihlukahlukeneko

umsetjenzana wokuhlola-umsetjenzana osetjenziswa ukuhlola abafundi ngemisetjenzana eminengana namkha
iingcenye

ifana-tjhada- kubuyabuyelela itjhada elifanako, ivamisa itjhada elingukamisa nanyana elingungwaqa. Ukubuyelela
kungabonakala ekuthomeni emagameni alandelanako nanyana ngaphakathi kwamagama

i-analoji - 1.ukuthola okufanako ezintweni ezibonwa njengezihlukileko.

2. ukuthola okufanako ezintweni ezibonakala njengezihlukileko, indlela yokuhlathulula nanyana yokutjengisa
okuthileko, kodwana ingasi ubufakazi. Umuntu kufanele atjheje i-analoji emamala. Kufanele kube nokukhambisana
kuhle phakathi kwento ehlathululwako kanye nemininingwana ye-analoji.

ukuveza umlingisi- ukucoca ngezehlakalo ezincani nanyana ukukhuluma
ngezehlakalo ngomnqopho wokuthula umbiko, ukujabulisa, nanyana ukuveza umlingisi

abopopayi- maqhinga wokusebenzisa umlandelande weenthombe ukwakha isithombe-ngqondo somnyakazo
somsikinyeko nanyana sepilo

irhobho-ndaba/ibohlololo – kungazaliseki kwesiphetho egade silindelwe, sekunalokho indaba yehliswe sisehlakalo
nje esingatjho litho nofana esilihlaya eliseqadi

bunqopha- indlela yokuveza imibono emibili nanyana ehlukileko elinganako (isib. Ukuzonda khulu, ngebelo elincani)

igama eliphikisako- ligama elitjho ihlathululo engafaniko neyelinye igama ngelimi elifanako (isib.’thaba’ kanye
‘notlhuwa’)

ngokufaneleko- nangabe ilimi lifanele lelo limi lifanelekile ngokuya kwetheksthi lapho lisetjenzliswe khona (isib.
Ukuthi nilale kuhle kuzakukhambelana nesikhathi sokulala kodwana ukuthi kusile kungakhambelana nokuphuma
kwelanga)

ukuhlola- ikambiso eragela phambili ehlelekileko yokubuthelela ilwazi elimayelana nekghono lomfundi ngeendlela
ezinengi ezihlukileko

Ukubuyelela abokamisa- 1. ukubuyelela khulukhulu kwabokamisa emagameni amabili nanyana emagameni
amanengi isib.”Khalakatjha”

2. ukwengeza- itjhada labokamisa akunafuneko bonyana linembe: ukubuyelela kungakha umlandelande wetjhada
labokamisa endaweni ethileko

abamukelilwazi – 1. mumuntu nanyana abantu abalalelako, abafundako, nanyana ababukela amatheksthi
ngomnqopho wokuthola ilwazi (Isib. abalaleli behlelo lomrhatjhi weendaba)

2. ukwengeza- khulukhulu, bamukeli-lwazi abakhambela imidlalo yeengoma nanyana umdlalo

itheksthi ephathekako- itheksthi enomsebenzi obonakalako begodu ayingabazeki (isib. imagazini namaphepha-
ndaba, ama-athikili, agadangiswe emirhatjhweni nakumabonwakude, imikhangiso, amabhrotjha wokuvakatjha,
iindlela zakarhulumende, iimbonelo zeencwadi eziliqiniso)

ubuhlangothi – kuthatha enye into nofana umbono ngendlela yokukhethulula nanyana ukuthanda elinye ihlangothi
nanyana umuntu kunomunye okwenza kube budisi ukobana kuhlolwe kuhle.

2. ukwengeza. Emdlalweni weenkotlelo, “isikhuni” siba budisi ngakwelinye ihlangothi bese senze ukobana sijikele
ngehlangothini lelo

okudosako- ngokuncani nanyana mbikwana otlolwe ngehla nanyana ngezasi ku-athikili, isithombe, njll.

ikhathuni- 1. ukuveza komlingisi ngendlela yokukhulisa amakghono wakhe nanyana ukuvela kwakhe.

2. ukwengeza- ukudweba ikhathuni ephepheni kwenzela ukurhwala nanyana ukuphula amatshwayo womlingisi

unobangela (qala umphumela)– lokho okubangela bona kube nokwenzekako nanyana ubujamo

amaqhinga wesinema – iinsetjenziswa esisetjenziswako nakwakhiwa ifilimu (isib. Ukubumbeka, umkhanyo,
umhlobo wokutjhuda)

hlathulula- ukwenza ihlathululo izwakale kuhle kumfundi

Umutjhwana- beka endaweni efaneleko. “indoda eyabe yembethe irhembhe ebovu yabaleka”. Umutjho oyihloko
ngothi, ”Indoda yabaleka”. Amagama athi, ‘yabe yembethe irhembhe ebovu ‘ mumutjho oyamileko’. Angeze wakghona
ukuzijamela uwodwa, nalokha isenzo siphelele. Imitjhwana eyamileko ithoma ngeenhlanganiso (begodu, nanyana)
nanyana nesabizwana senani (ngubani, yiphi). Isihlanganiso sihlanganisa umutjhwana nenye ingcenye yomutjho
oyihloko. “Indoda eyembethe irhembhe ebovu yabaleka”. Kilomutjho “ukwembatha irhembhe ebovu” akusiwo

 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
umutjhwana nanyana ibinzana lamagama. Isenzo asikapheleli (kulicezwana)

isitlhori/isiqongo – lizinga eliphezulu kunawo woke (lokugcina) lesehlakalo endabeni. Lelizinga liqakatheke khulu.
Sisehlakalo esizidlula zoke izehlakalo ngokuqakatheka okuthusako/okukarisako nanyana okwenzekako endabeni

ukukhambelana – 1. itjhebiswano elizwakalako elingabonakaliko elihlanganisa imibono ibumbane bese yenza bona
indima nanyana indinyana ibumbane

2. Ukungezelela: kungafaka nehlelo elaneleko ukuletha ihlathululo, nanyana ukuhlela imitjho ngokulandelana. Ihlelo
elinomraro lingenza isitatimende bona singakhambisani.

ukukhambisana –ukuhlangana nangabe imitjho, iindima ngokuhlanganiswa kuhle ngokufana neenhlanganiso,
izabizwana kanye nebuyelelo

ukuhlanganisa – kuhlanganisa imibono evela emithonjeni ehlukahlukeneko wakhe umbono munye ozwakalako
wemibono embalwa

ikholokhyalizimu (qala isirhumutjha) – ilimi eliba ngelekulumo engaziwa ngabanye nanyana elingakajayeleki
kodwana elingasetjenziswa elimini elihlelekileko

madanisako/-khulisako – mazinga wokumadanisa nokukhulisa (njengeemphawulo neembaluli u- “omkhulu”,
omkhudlwana”, “omkhulu khulu”

madanisa- (qala okungakhambisaniko) –ukuhlola indlela izinto ezifana ngayo

irarano- Kukulwa nanyana kuphikisana okuvela hlangana nabalingisi bodwa nanyana kwabalingisi nobujamo
obuthileko bezinto. Irarano lingavela ekuqhulaneni kwemicabango yomlingisi ayedwa.

isihlanganiso – ligama elisetjenziselwa ukuhlanganisa imitjhwana emibili, amagama, ibinzana kanye nemitjho

Isihlanganisi- ligama elisetjenziselwa ukuhlanganisa imitjhwana emibili, amagama, ibinzana nanyana imitjho

ihlathululo enqophileko(qala ihlathululo ebhamba) – kokubili ukuhlangana okuhle nokumbi igama elikubuthako
ngokukusebenzisa okudlula ihlathululo ebhamba (esisekelo)

ubujamo– itheksthi ngaso soke isikhathi iyasetjenziswa bese ikhiqizwa ngokobujamo, ubujamo bufaka hlangana
ubujamo obunabileko kanye nobumsinya kufakwe hlangana izinto ezifana nehlalakuhle, isiko kanye nesendlalelo
sepolotiki, ithemu lingatjho godu lokho okwenzeke ngaphambilini nanyana okulandela igama nanyana itheksthi
begodu kuqakathekile ehlathululweni yalo

okumumethweko – ngilokho okungaphakathi komtlolo

amatshwayo wobujamo – amatshwayo wobujamo kusebenzisa amagama akhambisana negama elingaziwako
ukunikela ihlathululo. Iqhingeli lokufunda lingafundiswa kanye nelwazi-magama.

ukumadanisa (qala indlela yokusetjenziswa kwelimi – ukutsengwa nanyana ukuhlolwa kwendlela ihlathululo
ebunjwa ngayo ngokuzwisisa amandla wetjhebiswano ngaphakathi nahlangana namalimi; kunikela umfundi amandla
ukobana abalekele ukudlelezelwa begodu asebenzise ilimi ngendlela efaneleko

(qala ukumadanisa) – ukuqala indlela izinto ezihlukana ngayo

imithetho yelimi – iindlela kanye nemithetho efaneleko yokusebenzisa ilimi . eminye imithetho isiza ukudlulisa

 ISINDEBELE ILIMI LOKUTHOMA LOKWENGEZA AMAGREYIDI 4-6
ihlathululo (isib. Imithetho yehlelo, amagabhadlhela); eminye isiza ekwethuleni lokho okumunyethweko (isib.
Okumunyethweko, isithombe esivamileko, iinhloko, ilwazi elingezeleleko elitlolwa phasi, amatjhadi, iinhloko,
amarhelo, iinthombe, i-indeksi); begodu eminye itjengisa iphetheni yelimi ebe esele litlanyiwe (isib. Ukulotjhisa,
ukukhuluma kancazana)

ukuhlola okuragela phambili- kufaka hlangana imisebenzi ehlolwako eyenziwa unyaka woke

ikulumo-pikiswano – kukulumo-pikiswano, iinqhema ezimbili ziphalisana ngokwazo. Umnqopho kutjhugulula ijaji
nabamukelilwazi ukobana umbonwabo ngesihloko abaphiwe sona uzwakala kuhle begodu ungofaneleko kunaloyo
wesinye isiqhema.

Ihlathululo ebhamba (qala godu ihlathululo engakanqophi) – yihlathululo yegama njengombana linjalo

Igama elisuselwe kwelinye – igama elisuselwe kwelinye nanyana emrabhini, esikhathini esinengi lakhiwa ngokufaka
isithomo nanyana isilungelelo isib. ‘umuntu’ ususelwa esiqwini ‘-ntu’)

ilimi lesigodi – indlela yelimi elisetjenziswa mphakathi othileko; lihluke khulu kunezinye iindlela zelimi elifana nalo
ngokwamagama, ukwakheka begodu/kanye nokuphimiswa kwamagama

itshimo lomdlalo – lenzeka lokha abamukelilwazi/ababukeli/abafundi/bazi izinto ezinengi ngobujamo kanye
nemiphumela yazo kunabalingisi ababandakanyekako, kwenza bona ukutjhuba komoya kurhagale, ukuthaba kanye
nokuzibandakanya kwabamukelilwazi

isakhiwo somdlalo – 1. Indlela ekhethekileko yesitayela semitlolo imidlalo etloleke ngayo

2. ukuhleleka kwesakhiwo, iinkundla, iinqephu, abadlali nakukghonakalako namatshwayo welimi emdlalweni.

ukufika esiphethweni; ukusebenzisa okutloliweko nanyana imitlhala ebonakalako ukubona lokho okungakavezwa
bunqopha nakafundako.

uku-editha –indlela yekambiso yokwenza itlhatlhabejo nanyana ukwenza ngobutjha itlhatlhabejo yetheksthi,
kufakwe hlangana ukulungiswa kwemitjhapho yelimi, amatshwayo nemitjhapho yokutloleka kwamagama nokuhlola
ukutlola ukobana imibono ikhambelane begodu nesakhiwo esikhambelanako, kunrhatjhi, uku-editha kufaka hlangana
ukwakha, ukukhetha nokutlama amatheksthi

umphumela (qala godu unobangela) –umphumela nanyana isiphetho sesenzo nanyana ubujamo

isithintela/umphumela – ngokwenza into ethileko ithinteke ngokhunye okwenzekako/umphumela wesehlakalo
esithileko

ilimi elithinta imizwa – ilimi elivusa imizwa ebukhali

ihlonipho– ukuveza ngendlela elula nanyana engazwakaliko ejanyiselelwa ngomcabango nanyana ngegama
elizwakala lilumela nanyana libunqopha

okubonakalako (ukungakhambisani nokungabonakali)- ihlathululo etjhatjhalazi nanyana evezwe bunqopha

ukuhlola kwangaphandle – kuhlolwa okwenzeka ngaphandle kwesikolo nanyana etlasini

hlola: bumba imibono, thatha iinqunto, kanye nokuthuthukisa imibono ekufundeni

isithombe-ngqondo – magama akha imifanekiso engqondweni (Isib: isifaniso, isingathekiso ukwenza-samuntu)
